Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the algorithms framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 35. The resulting operator achieves bound O(750/n^4).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to algorithms structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 35/750 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
